[booklet]: # "Weavers of the Void® booklet: the powers of a god"

# Weavers of the Void®: The Powers of a God

You play the Creator. The engine lives the world on its own, epoch after epoch, and your sons act on their own too. Nothing it makes is out of your reach: every person, people, place, creature, god, world and star, every value they hold and every mechanic that moves them can be made, changed, held or unmade by your hand. This booklet lists every one of those powers, what you choose when you use it, what it costs and where in the game you find it.

| Your powers at a glance | |
|---|---:|
| Powers | 2,666 |
| Chapters | 21 |
| Subjects | 857 |
| Free powers | 2,609 |
| Powers that cost essence | 57 |
| Powers that let you hold the result | 319 |
| Longer works that run in the background | 20 |
| Powers found on a screen of the game | 2,666 |

## How to read this booklet

- The powers are gathered into **chapters** by what they touch: the Creator, the sons, peoples, war, the cosmos and more.
- Within a chapter, powers on the same thing share a **subject**: making, changing and unmaking one kind of record sit together.
- **What you choose** lists what you pick or fill in when you use the power. *Optional* means you may leave it out.
- **Cost** is what the power takes from the Creator's essence. Costs follow your world's rules, so they change when you change those rules.
- **Where** names the screens and pages of the game that offer the power. A page opens over any screen when you pick something by name.
- The companion booklets *How the World Connects* and *The Rules of the World* describe the mechanics these powers reach and the rules they follow.

## What a god does

Most powers are one of a few deeds done to a kind of record.

| Deed | Powers | What it does |
|---|---:|---|
| Create | 268 | Bring a new one into the world. |
| Edit | 583 | Change any of its values. |
| Delete | 496 | Take it out of the world. |
| Set | 213 | Give it a value outright. |
| Remove | 69 | Take it away from where it is. |
| Release | 31 | Let a held value follow the world again. |
| Hold | 23 | Keep it as it is, so the engine cannot change it. |
| Run | 27 | Make the engine do it now. |
| Found | 21 | Found a new one. |
| Add | 19 | Add one to what is there. |
| Open | 14 | Open it. |
| Grant | 11 | Give it to someone. |
| End | 9 | Bring it to an end. |
| Other deeds | 882 | 588 rarer deeds and single powers, each named in its own power below. |

## Holding, undoing and the record

- **Holding.** 319 powers let you hold what you set. A held value or record stays as you left it: no step of the engine and no son may change it until you release it. 546 powers release held values again.
- **Pinning.** Any single value of any record can be pinned, so that no mechanic changes it, and released later.
- **Your ledger.** Every power you use is written into your ledger with what it changed. From the Ledger screen you can undo an entry, and the world goes back to how it was before it. A page cannot be torn out while an epoch is running.
- **Changes of the engine.** Every value the engine changes is recorded with its cause, and 2 powers set one back as it was.
- **Backups.** The world is saved before every epoch, and a saved world can be brought back from the settings.

### The sons

The sons are the gods you summon. Each acts on its own once an epoch. These powers summon, shape, guide, punish and reward them.

44 powers on 11 subjects.

#### Single powers

<a id="g-entities"></a>

### Things and beings

Every person, people, place, creature and object in a world is a thing. These powers move, change, mark and end things, and reach into their bodies and minds.

195 powers on 47 subjects.

## The lists you choose from

Where a power asks you to pick one of a list, the whole list is here.

<a id="e-accountkind"></a>

#### Account kind

14 choices: official chronicle, folk memory, foreign account, gospel, epic, inscription, founding myth, elder memory, record, testimony, genealogy, song, travel account, law record.

<a id="e-accountmedium"></a>

#### Account medium

8 choices: artwork, book, oral, inscription, record, performance, mind state, marks.

<a id="e-accountstance"></a>

#### Account stance

8 choices: glorify, vilify, omit, neutral, justify, lament, mock, warn.

<a id="e-actkind"></a>

#### Act kind

67 choices: execution, mutilation, betrayal, mercy to enemy, sacrifice of people, trade with outsiders, building temples, burning books, conquest, raiding, duelling, ransom, taking captives, oathbreaking, learning, hospitality, winter fighting, guest killing, ritual scarring, killing kin, killing outsider, killing in war, killing captive, regicide, duel to death, revenge killing, theft, treason, cowardice, desecration, bribery, suicide, torture, eating sapients, iconoclasm, conversion by force, polygamy, incest, magic use, blood magic, void magic, necromancy, hoarding in famine, slavery, adultery, usury, infanticide, abandoning elders, forced labour, selling offices, poaching, enclosing, lying, drunkenness, tomb robbing, using charms, feeding on hosts, killing sacred animal, fixing contests, abandoning the sick, breaking hospitality, cruelty to animals, apologising, sheltering strangers, laying curses, enhancing contestants, poison wells.

<a id="e-adoptionkind"></a>

#### Adoption kind

6 choices: heir, ward, foster, sponsor, blood bond, chosen kin.

<a id="e-affinitymode"></a>

#### Affinity mode

4 choices: consumed, worn, inscribed, ground.

<a id="e-afterlifemodel"></a>

#### Afterlife model

9 choices: judged realms, common realm, realm by death, realm of line, rebirth, wandering, union, oblivion, among living.

<a id="e-ageband"></a>

#### Age band

6 choices: infant, child, youth, adult, elder, ancient.

<a id="e-agecycle"></a>

#### Age cycle

3 choices: linear, cyclic, spiral.

<a id="e-agegrade"></a>

#### Age grade

6 choices: children, youths, young adults, junior elders, senior elders, ancients.

<a id="e-agekind"></a>

#### Age kind

10 choices: normal, golden, dark, heroic, rising, declining, transforming, stagnant, wandering, reborn.

<a id="e-aidkind"></a>

#### Aid kind

11 choices: goods, healers, labour, money, credit, food, shelter, medicine, soldiers, knowledge, transport.

<a id="e-allocationmode"></a>

#### Allocation mode

8 choices: market, redistribution, command, obligation, gift, need, abundance, lottery.

<a id="e-animalrole"></a>

#### Animal role

19 choices: draft, mount, dairy, fibre, meat, guard, hunting, war, companion, sacred, pest control, messenger, sensing, entertainment, reagent source, sport, medicine, display, herding.

<a id="e-anomalykind"></a>

#### Anomaly kind

6 choices: failed prediction, contrary cure, observation, divine sign, contrary record, out of place find.

<a id="e-apocalypsevariant"></a>

#### Apocalypse variant

6 choices: ruin, world war, outer descent, end of the gods, dimensional collapse, galactic death.

<a id="e-appealkind"></a>

#### Appeal kind

8 choices: belief, self interest, authority, evidence, fear, feeling, bond, tradition.

<a id="e-arcrole"></a>

#### Arc role

9 choices: rival, mentor, beloved, betrayer, heir, foe, ally, witness, guide.

<a id="e-arcstage"></a>

#### Arc stage

11 choices: inciting, rising, crisis, climax, call, refusal, mentor, threshold, ordeal, return, resolution.

<a id="e-arctemplate"></a>

#### Arc template

22 choices: rise and fall, rags to riches, revenge, redemption, quest, tragedy, forbidden love, rivalry, mentor and heir, exile and return, corruption, coming of age, fall of a house, founding, apotheosis, overcoming, reconciliation, sacrifice, discovery, betrayal, friendship, legacy.

<a id="e-armsrim"></a>

#### Arms rim

6 choices: line, band, double, beaded, studded, rope.

<a id="e-arrivaloutcome"></a>

#### Arrival outcome

3 choices: settlers, refugees, invaders.

<a id="e-artmarketrole"></a>

#### Art market role

9 choices: collector, dealer, patron, connoisseur, curator, critic, forger, restorer, auctioneer.

<a id="e-artmode"></a>

#### Art mode

19 choices: verbal, musical, movement, flat image, solid image, built, worn, culinary, scent, light, recorded, interactive, landscape, body, ritual performance, textile image, made mind art, tactile, mind.

<a id="e-assistiveaid"></a>

#### Assistive aid

8 choices: spectacles, magnifier, telescope, hearing horn, cane, microscope, hearing aid, tactile script.

<a id="e-atmosphere"></a>

#### Atmosphere

7 choices: none, thin, normal, dense, toxic, steam, layered.

<a id="e-attackdelivery"></a>

#### Attack delivery

12 choices: breath, spit, spray, emanation, gaze, voice, touch, spores, bite, sting, projectile, constriction.

<a id="e-attackeffect"></a>

#### Attack effect

13 choices: heat, cold, corrosion, shock, venom, force, entangle, fear, stillness, sleep, confusion, charm, drain.

<a id="e-attackrange"></a>

#### Attack range

5 choices: touch, near, far, sight, area.

<a id="e-attendancereason"></a>

#### Attendance reason

8 choices: host, kin, friend, obligation, faith, renown, neighbour, summoned.

<a id="e-augmentkind"></a>

#### Augment kind

10 choices: replacement, enhancement, interface, weapon, regulator, sense, storage, cosmetic, life support, armour.

<a id="e-augmentmake"></a>

#### Augment make

4 choices: made, rite bound, grown, power bound.
